QA Regression Testing Test inbound 834 files with varying combinations of Eligibility Date, Assessment Date changes, and relevant field changes to verify correct transaction effective date population. Verify no change in capitation calculations and accurate reporting for finance team. Confirm that Maintenance Date is only used when specified in acceptance criteria. | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 does not use Maintenance Date to split spans for a standard update when Maintenance Date is not the allowed driver | TC_TS006_002 | None | Negative | Functional | Verify for AR PASSE inbound 834 standard update processing that Maintenance Date in Loop 2000 does not create a maintenance-date-driven split or transaction-effective-date override when the update does not meet the limited condition where Maintenance Date is allowed. | Applicable state and trading partner for this story is AR PASSE. A member already exists in FACPRDDB with an active eligibility span effective before the incoming change date, and the member SSN used in NM109 is available as [TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file]. Prepare one AR PASSE update file for the same member with current eligibility already active, with Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date set to 20240901, Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date set to 20240910, and Loop 2000 DTP01 maintenance date set to 20240920. The changed field in the file must represent a normal update that is not one of the limited cases where maintenance date is permitted to drive span handling. The file must be uniquely named and ready in \\daycrtappfs01\EdifecsSTRoot\834\Inbound. | 14 | Open a new SSMS session using Database Engine and connect to crt_72.sql.caresource.corp\crt_72 for auxiliary-span validation. | Connection to crt_72.sql.caresource.corp\crt_72 succeeds. | None |
